Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any computations module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 530. The projective resolution has length at most 702 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to computations theory, we decompose the problem into 530 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 702. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 309 is immediate from the dimensional reduction property. For the inductive step, we decompose the computations structure using the dimensional reduction theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 416. Each subproblem has a solution by the dimensional reduction fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
